<p>The platform supports the full structured product lifecycle—from idea generation and price discovery through to primary issuance, secondary trading, and lifecycle management. As a core tool for both our structured products sales team and their clients, Marquee Structured Products streamlines trading workflows, delivering greater simplicity, transparency, and efficiency.</p>

class="gs-uitk-c-127bxnm--text-root--top-spacing-style-top-spacing--bottom-spacing-style-bottom-spacing--spacing-style-bottom-spacing--card-card-body"><p>Risk sentiment bounced back to pre-war levels in May, as markets looked past Middle East risks. But volatile headlines around the Strait of Hormuz is leading to an uneven sentiment across asset classes.</p>
